Every ship to which this Code applies shall have on board a valid Polar Ship Certificate.
Normalized requirement:
IF polar_code_applies = true, THEN polar_ship_certificate_present = true AND polar_ship_certificate_status = valid.

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .

    ) .

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeApplies ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship must record that the Polar Code applies." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarShipCertificatePresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "A ship to which the Polar Code applies must have a Polar Ship Certificate." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarShipCertificateStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate must be recorded as valid." ;
    ] .

gen:IMO014NonApplicableBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeApplies ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "When the Polar Code applies, polarCodeApplies must be true."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarShipCertificatePresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "A valid Polar Ship Certificate must be present when the Polar Code applies."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarShipCertificateStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate status must be valid when the Polar Code applies."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarCodeApplies ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "polarCodeApplies must be false when the Polar Code does not apply."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Clause / section / page: 4.4.4.2 / Support of frames against instability, in particular tripping / 21
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-052-20260915T131355709888Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Asymmetrical frames and frames which are not at right angles to the shell (web less than 90 degrees to the shell) shall be supported against tripping by brackets, intercoastals, stringers or similar, at a distance not exceeding 1,300 mm. For frames with spans greater than 4 m, the extent of antitripping supports must be applied to all regions and for all ice classes. For frames with spans less than or equal to 4 m, the extent of antitripping supports must be applied to all regions for ice class IA Super, to the bow and midbody regions for ice class IA, and to the bow region for ice classes IB and IC. Direct calculation methods may be applied to demonstrate the equivalent level of support provided by alternative arrangements.
Normalized requirement:
IF frame_asymmetrical = true OR frame_web_angle_to_shell_deg < 90, THEN antitripping_support_spacing_mm <= 1300. IF frame_span_m > 4, THEN antitripping_support_scope = 'all regions/all ice classes'. IF frame_span_m <= 4, THEN required scope is all regions for IA Super, bow and midbody for IA, and bow for IB or IC. An alternative arrangement is acceptable only when equivalent_support_status_by_direct_calculation = demonstrated.
Required inputs: iceClass

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF052FrameShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF052AlternativeArrangementShape
        gen:TRF052StandardArrangementShape
    ) .

gen:TRF052AlternativeArrangementShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:equivalentSupportStatusByDirectCalculation ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] .

gen:TRF052StandardArrangementShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameAsymmetrical ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameWebAngleToShell ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF052AngleQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameSpan ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF052SpanQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        )
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:frameAsymmetrical ?asym .
                        $this nltl:frameWebAngleToShell ?angleNode .
                        ?angleNode qudt:numericValue ?angle .
                        FILTER ( ! (?asym = true || ?angle < 90) )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:antitrippingSupportSpacing ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:TRF052SpacingQuantityShape
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The antitripping support regions required for the frame span and ice class are incomplete." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:frameSpan ?spanNode .
                ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ice .

                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?span > 4 &&
                        !(
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:bowRegion } &&
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:midbodyRegion } &&
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:sternRegion } &&
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:otherIceStrengthenedAreaValue }
                        )
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?span <= 4 &&
                        ?ice = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                        !(
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:bowRegion } &&
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:midbodyRegion } &&

                        )
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?span <= 4 &&
                        ?ice = nltl:iceClassIa &&
                        !(
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:bowRegion } &&
                            EXISTS { $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:midbodyRegion }
                        )
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?span <= 4 &&
                        (?ice = nltl:iceClassIb || ?ice = nltl:iceClassIc) &&
                        !EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasAntitrippingSupportRegion nltl:bowRegion
                        }
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF052SpacingQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
```

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-052-FrameSupportShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:frame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameAsymmetrical ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Frame asymmetry status is required to determine tripping-support applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameWebAngleToShell ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            a sh:NodeShape ;

                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;

            ]
        ] ;

    ] ;

        sh:path nltl:frameSpan ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;

                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Frame span is required in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ice class is required to determine the applicable support region."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:equivalentSupportStatusByDirectCalculation ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "An applicable frame requires antitripping support spacing not exceeding 1300 mm, unless equivalent support is demonstrated by direct calculation." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:equivalentSupportStatusByDirectCalculation true
                    }
                )
                {
                    $this nltl:frameAsymmetrical true
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:frameWebAngleToShell ?angleNode .
                    ?angleNode qudt:numericValue ?angle .
                    ?angleNode qudt:unit unit:DEG .
                    FILTER (?angle < 90)
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:antitrippingSupportSpacing ?spacingNode .
                    ?spacingNode qudt:numericValue ?spacing .
                    ?spacingNode qudt:unit unit:MilliM .
                    FILTER (?spacing <= 1300)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For frames with spans greater than 4 m, antitripping support must cover the bow, midbody, and stern regions." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
```

Original source text:
Open water means a large area of freely navigable water in which sea ice is present in concentrations less than 1/10. No ice of land origin is present.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_condition = 'open water', THEN sea_ice_concentration_tenths < 1 AND ice_of_land_origin_present = false.
Required inputs: Ice concentration and land-origin-ice observation

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO_007_OpenWaterDefinitionShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceConditionClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:message "A ship shall record an ice condition classification."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the ice condition is open water, sea ice concentration shall be a numeric value less than 1 with unitless units, and ice of land origin shall be recorded as false." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceConditionClassification nltl:openWaterIceCondition .
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:seaIceConcentration ?concentration .
                        ?concentration qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                                       qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                        FILTER (
                            ?value < 1
                        )
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:seaIceConcentration ?concentration .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?concentration qudt:numericValue ?value .
                            FILTER (
                                ?value < 1
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:seaIceConcentration ?concentration .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?concentration qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:seaIceConcentration ?concentration .
                        ?concentration qudt:unit ?unit .
                        FILTER (?unit != unit:UNITLESS)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:iceOfLandOriginPresent ?present .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?present) = xsd:boolean &&
                            ?present = false
                        )
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:iceOfLandOriginPresent ?present .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            FILTER (
                                datatype(?present) = xsd:boolean &&
                                ?present = false
                            )
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_007_OpenWaterShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceConditionClassification ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:openWaterIceCondition ;
                sh:message "The ship's ice condition classification must identify open water for the open-water branch."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:seaIceConcentration ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxExclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:message "Sea ice concentration must be less than 1 for open water."
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS ;
                        sh:message "Sea ice concentration must use the unitless unit."
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceOfLandOriginPresent ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "Ice of land origin must be absent for open water."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceConditionClassification ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB
                    nltl:iceFreeIceCondition
                    nltl:mediumFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions
                    nltl:thinFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions
                    nltl:otherWatersIceCondition
                ) ;
                sh:message "The ship's ice condition classification must identify a permitted non-open-water condition."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 54
Batch: BATCH_06
Requirement ID: TRF-044

Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 4.4.2.1 / Section modulus and shear area / 18-19
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-044-20260915T130123817775Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The section modulus of a main or intermediate transverse frame shall be calculated using Z = p*s*h*l/(m_t*sigma_y)*10^6 [cm^3] (4.7), and the effective shear area shall be calculated using A = sqrt(3)*f_3*p*h*s/(2*sigma_y)*10^4 [cm^2] (4.8), where p is the ice pressure from 4.2.2, s is the frame spacing, h is the load-area height from 4.2.1, l is the frame span, m_t = 7*m_0/(7 - 5*h/l), f_3 = 1.2, sigma_y is the yield stress from 4.3.2, and m_0 is selected from Table 4-7 for the applicable boundary condition. Table 4-7 m0 values: bulk carrier frames with top wing tanks 7; frames from tank top to main deck of a single-decked vessel 6; continuous frames between several decks or stringers 5.7; frames between two decks only 5.
Normalized requirement:
Compute m_t = 7*m_0/(7 - 5*h/l), Z_cm3 = p_MPa*s_m*h_m*l_m/(m_t*sigma_y_N_mm2)*10^6, and A_cm2 = sqrt(3)*1.2*p_MPa*h_m*s_m/(2*sigma_y_N_mm2)*10^4. Select m_0 from Table 4-7 for the frame boundary condition. Table 4-7 m0 values: bulk carrier frames with top wing tanks 7; frames from tank top to main deck of a single-decked vessel 6; continuous frames between several decks or stringers 5.7; frames between two decks only 5.
Required inputs: yieldStrength_MPa, sectionModulusZ, shearAreaA

```turtle
                BIND((1.7320508075688772 * 1.2 * ?p * ?h * ?s / (2 * ?sy) * 10000) AS ?expectedA)

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?mt - ?expectedMt) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedMt) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?z - ?expectedZ) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedZ) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?a - ?expectedA) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedA) + 0.000001)
```

```turtle
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameMomentFactorMt ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sectionModulus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:CentiM3 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:requiredShearArea ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:CentiM2 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The boundary-condition m0 table value or one of the calculated results is non-conforming; calculated decimal results use relative tolerance 1e-6 plus absolute fallback 1e-9." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:frameBoundaryConditionType ?condition ;
                      nltl:icePressure ?pNode ;
                      nltl:spacing ?sNode ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?hNode ;
                      nltl:span ?lNode ;
                      nltl:frameBoundaryConditionFactorM0 ?m0Node ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yNode ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorMt ?mtNode ;
                      nltl:sectionModulus ?zNode ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea ?aNode .

                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?sNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?hNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?lNode qudt:numericValue ?l .
                ?m0Node qudt:numericValue ?m0 .
                ?yNode qudt:numericValue ?y .
                ?mtNode qudt:numericValue ?mt .
                ?zNode qudt:numericValue ?z .
                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .

                BIND(
                    IF(
                        ?condition = nltl:bulkCarrierTopWingTankFrameCondition,
                        7.0,
                        IF(
                            ?condition = nltl:singleDeckTankTopToMainDeckFrameCondition,
                            6.0,
                            IF(
                                ?condition = nltl:multiDeckOrStringerContinuousFrameCondition,
                                5.7,
                                5.0
                            )
                        )
                    )
                    AS ?m0Expected
                )
                BIND(7.0 * ?m0Expected / (7.0 - 5.0 * ?h / ?l) AS ?mtExpected)
                BIND(?p * ?s * ?h * ?l / (?mtExpected * ?y) * 1000000.0 AS ?zExpected)
                BIND(1.7320508075688772 * 1.2 * ?p * ?h * ?s / (2.0 * ?y) * 10000.0 AS ?aExpected)

                FILTER(
                    ?m0 != ?m0Expected ||
                    ABS(?mt - ?mtExpected) >
                        (ABS(?mtExpected) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001) ||
                    ABS(?z - ?zExpected) >
                        (ABS(?zExpected) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001) ||
                    ABS(?a - ?aExpected) >
                        (ABS(?aExpected) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-104-CrewMemberShape>
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-104-CrewMemberShape>
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Each crew member shall have a familiarization record for every PWOM procedure or equipment item relevant to the crew member's assigned duties." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ?crewMember .
                ?crewMember nltl:hasAssignedDuty ?duty .
                ?duty nltl:hasRelevantPolarWaterOperationalManualItem ?item .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?crewMember nltl:hasFamiliarizationRecord ?record .
                    ?record nltl:familiarizationRecordItem ?item .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_104_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarWaterOperationalManualFamiliarizationStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall record completed PWOM familiarization."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ;
        sh:class nltl:crewMember
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each crew member's assigned-duty-relevant PWOM item shall have a corresponding familiarization record item." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ?crewMember .
                ?crewMember nltl:hasAssignedDuty ?assignedDuty .
                ?assignedDuty nltl:hasRelevantPolarWaterOperationalManualItem ?manualItem .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?crewMember nltl:hasFamiliarizationRecord ?record .
                    ?record nltl:familiarizationRecordItem ?manualItem .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO_104_CrewMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetObjectsOf nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ;
    sh:class nltl:crewMember ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasAssignedDuty ;
        sh:class nltl:assignedDuty
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasFamiliarizationRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:familiarizationRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each crew member shall have a PWOM familiarization record."
    ] .

gen:IMO_104_AssignedDutyShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetObjectsOf nltl:hasAssignedDuty ;
    sh:class nltl:assignedDuty ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRelevantPolarWaterOperationalManualItem ;
        sh:class nltl:polarWaterOperationalManualItem
    ] .

gen:IMO_104_FamiliarizationRecordShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetObjectsOf nltl:hasFamiliarizationRecord ;
    sh:class nltl:familiarizationRecord ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:familiarizationRecordItem ;
        sh:class nltl:polarWaterOperationalManualItem
    ] .

```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-004-20260915T102320414882Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
First-year ice means sea ice of not more than one winter growth developing from young ice with thickness from 0.3 m to 2.0 m.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_origin = 'sea ice' AND winter_growth_count <= 1 AND 0.3 <= ice_thickness_m <= 2.0, THEN ice_type = 'first-year ice'.
Required inputs: Ice origin, winter growth history, and measured thickness

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO_004_StaticShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceOriginType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:winterGrowthCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceThickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the ice is sea ice with no more than one winter growth and thickness from 0.3 m through 2.0 m, the ice type shall be first-year ice." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceOriginType nltl:seaIceOrigin ;
                      nltl:winterGrowthCount ?growth ;
                      nltl:iceThickness ?thicknessNode .
                ?thicknessNode qudt:numericValue ?thickness ;
                                qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (
                    ?growth <= 1 &&
                    ?thickness >= 0.3 &&
                    ?thickness <= 2.0
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceTypeClassification nltl:firstYearIce .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the applicable first-year-ice conditions hold, ice type classification shall not contain a conflicting value." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceOriginType nltl:seaIceOrigin ;
                      nltl:winterGrowthCount ?growth ;
                      nltl:iceThickness ?thicknessNode .
                ?thicknessNode qudt:numericValue ?thickness ;
                                qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (
                    ?growth <= 1 &&
                    ?thickness >= 0.3 &&
                    ?thickness <= 2.0
                )
                $this nltl:iceTypeClassification ?classification .
                FILTER (?classification != nltl:firstYearIce)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-004_IceTypeClassificationShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the ship has sea ice of no more than one winter growth and thickness from 0.3 m through 2.0 m, it must be classified as first-year ice." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceOriginType nltl:seaIceOrigin ;
                       nltl:winterGrowthCount ?growthCount ;
                       nltl:iceThickness ?thicknessNode .

                ?thicknessNode qudt:numericValue ?thickness ;
                                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .

                FILTER (
                    datatype(?growthCount) = xsd:integer &&
                    ?growthCount <= 1 &&
                    datatype(?thickness) = xsd:decimal &&
                    ?thickness >= 0.3 &&
                    ?thickness <= 2.0
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceTypeClassification nltl:firstYearIce .
                }
            }
        """
```

Clause / section / page: 1.2.7 / Part I-A Chapter 1 - Definitions / 13

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Original source text:
Maximum expected time of rescue means the time adopted for the design of equipment and system that provide survival support. It shall never be less than 5 days.
Normalized requirement:
maximum_expected_rescue_time_days >= 5.
Required inputs: Adopted maximum expected rescue time

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-015> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:bowSubregionCalculationCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:bowFormApplicabilityClassification ;
                sh:class nltl:bowFormApplicabilityValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each applicable I2.3.1(v) bow subregion case must contain the declared calculation inputs, lookup factors, and externally computed bow shape coefficient with the required quantity units." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:bowFormApplicabilityClassification nltl:i231vBowFormApplicability .

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:upperIceWaterlineLengthLUI ?lui .
                    ?lui qudt:numericValue ?luiValue ;
                         qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                    ?luiValue a xsd:decimal .

                    $this nltl:betaIPrime ?betaPrime .
                    ?betaPrime qudt:numericValue ?betaValue ;
                               qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    ?betaValue a xsd:decimal .

                    ?case nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?midPosition .
                    ?midPosition qudt:numericValue ?midValue ;
                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                    ?midValue a xsd:decimal .

                    ?case nltl:bowSubregionWaterlineAngle ?angle .
                    ?angle qudt:numericValue ?angleValue ;
                           qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG> .
                    ?angleValue a xsd:decimal .

                    ?case nltl:bowShapeCoefficient ?shapeCoefficient .
                    ?shapeCoefficient qudt:numericValue ?shapeValue ;
                                      qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    ?shapeValue a xsd:decimal .

                    ?case nltl:hasFailureClassFactorLookupCase ?lookupCase .
                    ?lookupCase nltl:flexuralFailureClassFactor ?flexuralFactor .
                    ?flexuralFactor qudt:numericValue ?flexuralValue ;
                                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    ?flexuralValue a xsd:decimal .

                    ?lookupCase nltl:crushingFailureClassFactor ?crushingFactor .
                    ?crushingFactor qudt:numericValue ?crushingValue ;
                                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    ?crushingValue a xsd:decimal .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
f_2 = 0.4/(h/s) + 0.6 where h/s <= 1; f_2 = 1.4 - 0.4*(h/s) where 1 <= h/s <= 1.8. Here h is as given in section 4.2.1 and sigma_y is the yield stress of the material [N/mm^2]. Use sigma_y = 235 N/mm^2 for normal-strength hull structural steel and sigma_y = 315 N/mm^2 or higher for high-strength hull structural steel. If steels with different yield stress are used, actual accepted values may be substituted. The abrasion and corrosion increment t_c shall normally be 2 mm; a lower approved value may be used for a suitable maintained special surface coating.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:TRF_042_PlatingShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:plating ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceLoadHeight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minExclusive 0
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "designIceLoadHeight must be a positive quantity in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:spacing ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minExclusive 0
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "spacing must be a positive quantity in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullStructuralSteelGrade ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:normalStrengthHullStructuralSteel
            nltl:highStrengthHullStructuralSteel
            nltl:steelGradeB
            nltl:steelGradeD
            nltl:steelGradeE
            nltl:steelGradeAh
            nltl:steelGradeDh
            nltl:steelGradeEh
            nltl:steelGradeFh
        ) ;
        sh:message "hullStructuralSteelGrade must identify an allowed normal-strength or high-strength hull structural steel grade."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:yieldStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minExclusive 0
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "yieldStrength must be a positive quantity in MPa."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalPlatingFactorF2 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "longitudinalPlatingFactorF2 must be a dimensionless quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:corrosionAbrasionAddition ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minExclusive 0 ;
                sh:maxInclusive 2
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MilliM>
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "corrosionAbrasionAddition must be a positive quantity not exceeding 2 mm."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "yieldStrength must represent 235 MPa for normal-strength hull structural steel, at least 315 MPa for high-strength hull structural steel, or a positive accepted substituted yield-stress value for the identified steel grade." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hullStructuralSteelGrade ?grade ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldNode .
                ?yieldNode qudt:numericValue ?yield .
                FILTER (datatype(?yield) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (
                    ?yield <= 0 ||
                    (
                        ?grade IN (
                            nltl:normalStrengthHullStructuralSteel,
                            nltl:steelGradeB,
                            nltl:steelGradeD,
                            nltl:steelGradeE
                        )
                        && ?yield != 235
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?grade IN (
                            nltl:highStrengthHullStructuralSteel,
                            nltl:steelGradeAh,
                            nltl:steelGradeDh,
                            nltl:steelGradeEh,
                            nltl:steelGradeFh
                        )
                        && ?yield < 315
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "longitudinalPlatingFactorF2 must follow the applicable piecewise formula; derived decimal comparison uses max(1e-6*ABS(expected),1e-12)." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?hNode ;
                      nltl:spacing ?sNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalPlatingFactorF2 ?fNode .
                ?hNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?sNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?fNode qudt:numericValue ?f .
                FILTER (datatype(?h) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (datatype(?s) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (datatype(?f) = xsd:decimal)
                BIND (?h / ?s AS ?ratio)
                BIND (
                    IF(
                        ?ratio <= 1,
                        0.4 / ?ratio + 0.6,
                        1.4 - 0.4 * ?ratio
                    ) AS ?expected
                )
                FILTER (
                    ?ratio > 1.8 ||
                    ABS(?f - ?expected) >
                    IF(
                        1e-6 * ABS(?expected) > 1e-12,
                        1e-6 * ABS(?expected),
                        1e-12
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A corrosion and abrasion addition below 2 mm requires a maintained special surface coating and exactly one material approval status value of nltl:evidenceStateApproved." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:corrosionAbrasionAddition ?tcNode .
                ?tcNode qudt:numericValue ?tc .
                FILTER (datatype(?tc) = xsd:decimal)
                FILTER (?tc < 2)
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:specialSurfaceCoatingMaintained true
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:materialApprovalStatus nltl:evidenceStateApproved
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:materialApprovalStatus ?suppliedStatus .
                        FILTER (?suppliedStatus != nltl:evidenceStateApproved)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
    sh:property [
```

```turtle
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The longitudinal plating factor f2 must satisfy the declared piecewise formula within max(1e-6*ABS(expected),1e-12)." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?hNode ;
                      nltl:spacing ?sNode ;
                      nltl:longitudinalPlatingFactorF2 ?fNode .
                ?hNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?sNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?fNode qudt:numericValue ?f .
                FILTER (
                    ?s = 0 ||
                    (
                        (?h / ?s) > 1.8 ||
                        ABS(
                            ?f -
                            IF(

                                1.4 - 0.4 * (?h / ?s)

                            ABS(

                                    (?h / ?s) <= 1,

                                    1.4 - 0.4 * (?h / ?s)
                                )
                            ) * 1e-6 > 1e-12,
                            ABS(
                                IF(
                                    (?h / ?s) <= 1,
                                    0.4 / (?h / ?s) + 0.6,
                                    1.4 - 0.4 * (?h / ?s)
                                )
                            ) * 1e-6,
                            1e-12
                        )
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A corrosion and abrasion addition below 2 mm requires a maintained special surface coating and approved material evidence." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:corrosionAbrasionAddition ?tcNode .
                ?tcNode qudt:numericValue ?tc .
                FILTER (?tc < 2)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:specialSurfaceCoatingMaintained true .
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:corrosionAbrasionAddition ?tcNode2 ;
                          nltl:specialSurfaceCoatingMaintained true ;
                          nltl:materialApprovalStatus ?status .
                    ?tcNode2 qudt:numericValue ?tc2 .
                    FILTER (
                        ?tc2 < 2 &&
                        ?status != nltl:evidenceStateApproved
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```
